# Company and Accounting Setup Guide

> **Goal**: Register 4ward4u as a trading entity with a complete accounting infrastructure so deals can be captured, confirmed, and accounted for.
>
> **Starting state**: 4ward4u Organization Business Partner already created. No Company, no GL Accounts, no Nostro Accounts exist yet.
>
> **Total records to create**: ~28 across 8 apps.
>
> **Estimated time**: 45–60 minutes (UI entry).

---

## Step 1.1 — Create the Company Record

**App**: Companies
**Action**: Create, fill fields, save

This is the legal entity. Everything belongs to a company.

### Identity tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Company Code | `4W4U` | Unique, UPPERCASE |
| Tenant Code | `4W4U` | Same as company code |
| Legal Name | `4ward4u (Pty) Ltd` | Full legal name |
| Trading Name | `4ward4u` | Display name |
| Registration Number | `2024/123456/07` | CIPC format |
| SARB Registration Number | `SARB-4W4U-001` | Leave blank if not a bank |
| VAT Registration Number | `4123456789` | 10-digit VAT number |
| Legal Entity Identifier | `5493001KJTIIGC8Y1R12` | 20-char LEI |

### Regulatory tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Institution Type | `Corporate Treasury` | Not a registered bank |
| Home Jurisdiction | `ZAF` | South Africa |
| Good Standing | `true` | Good standing |
| Exchange Control Authorised Dealer | `false` | Not an authorised dealer |

### Financial Identity tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Base Currency | `ZAR` | South African Rand |
| Fiscal Year Start | `03-01` | 1 March (SA tax year) |
| Fiscal Year End | `02-28` | 28 February |

### Operating Model tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Operating Model | `Treasury Only` | Pure treasury desk |
| Risk Structure Model | `Centralized` | All risk at group level |
| Accounting Basis | `Accrual` | Accrual accounting |
| Complexity Level | `Intermediate` | Multi-currency |
| Onboarding Status | `Completed` | Onboarding done |
| Client Business Enabled | `false` | Own book only |
| Default Acting Capacity | `Own Book` | Own book trading |
| Environment | `Development` | Dev environment |
| Go-Live Date | *(leave blank)* | Set when ready |
| Board Approval Reference | *(leave blank)* | Set when approved |

### Settlement tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| BIC / SWIFT Code | `4W4UZAJJ` | 8 or 11 chars |
| SAMOS Member ID | `4W4U001` | SAMOS member (if applicable) |
| Timezone | `Africa/Johannesburg` | SA timezone |

### Lifecycle tab
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Demo Company | `true` | Demo company |
| Status | `Onboarding` | Start in onboarding |
| Business Partner | *(select 4ward4u BP)* | Link to existing BP |
| Jurisdiction | `ZAF` | ZAF jurisdiction |

**After saving**: Note the Company UUID. You'll need it for every subsequent step.

---

## Step 1.2 — Company Feature Enablements

**App**: Company Feature Enablements
**Action**: Create, fill fields, save

This controls which readiness gates are enforced. In development, we bypass everything so we can test freely.

| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Company | *(select 4ward4u Company)* | FK to Company |
| Environment | `Development` | Dev environment |
| Readiness Gate Enforcement | `Informational` | Warnings only, no blocks |
| Bypass Readiness Gates | `true` | Skip readiness checks |
| Bypass Readiness Gates Expiry | `2026-10-18` | Within 30-day limit |
| Bypass Policy Gates | `true` | Skip policy checks |
| Bypass Policy Gates Expiry | `2026-10-18` | Within 30-day limit |
| Bypass Know Your Customer Gate | `true` | Skip KYC checks |
| Bypass Know Your Customer Gate Expiry | `2026-10-18` | Within 30-day limit |
| Bypass Business Window | `true` | Skip business hours |
| Bypass Business Window Expiry | `2026-10-18` | Within 30-day limit |
| Bypass Market Data | `true` | Skip market data |
| Bypass Market Data Expiry | `2026-10-18` | Within 30-day limit |

---

## Step 1.3 — General Ledger Accounts

**App**: General Ledger Accounts
**Action**: Create 8 GL accounts (one at a time)

These are the company's chart of accounts. The accounting engine posts to these.

**Common fields for ALL accounts**:
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Company | `4ward4u (Pty) Ltd` | Select from dropdown |
| CBS GL Code | *(leave blank)* | External system GL mapping |

---

### Account 1 — ZAR Nostro

**Account Definition**

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| GL Code | `GL-ZAR-NOSTRO` |
| Account Name | ZAR Nostro Account |
| Account Type | `Balance Sheet` |
| Account Group | `Client Account Group` |
| Normal Balance | `Debit` |
| Currency | `Rand (ZAR)` |
| Management Ledger Only | `false` |
| Active | `true` |

**Regulatory Classification**

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| IAS 7 Classification | `Amortised Cost` |
| HQLA Level | `Level 1` |
| SARB BA700 Line | `Liquid Assets Schedule` |
| FRTB Book Type | `Banking Book` |
| Tax Treatment | `Taxable` |
| OCI Recycling Rule | `Never Recycles` |
| XVA Applicable | `false` |
| ECL Provisioning | `true` |
| EIR Applicable | `true` |

**Approval**

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Approved By | *(leave blank)* |
| Approval Reference | *(leave blank)* |

## Step 1.4 — Company Standard Financial Nodes

**App**: Company Standard Financial Nodes
**Action**: Create 6 company financial nodes

These map the global financial node concepts to your company. Each node links to a Global Standard Financial Node and gets a company-specific node code.

| # | Company Node Code | Global Financial Node | Permitted Paths | Effective From | Active |
|---|------------------|----------------------|----------------|----------------|--------|
| 1 | `4W4U-FXFWD-ASSET` | FX Forward Asset | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |
| 2 | `4W4U-FXFWD-LIAB` | FX Forward Liability | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |
| 3 | `4W4U-FXFWD-SETTLE-DR` | FX Forward Settlement Debit | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |
| 4 | `4W4U-FXFWD-SETTLE-CR` | FX Forward Settlement Credit | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |
| 5 | `4W4U-FX-UNREAL-GAIN` | FX Unrealized Gain | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |
| 6 | `4W4U-FX-UNREAL-LOSS` | FX Unrealized Loss | `Own Book` | `2026-01-01` | `true` |

**Common fields for all nodes**:
| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Company | *(select 4ward4u Company)* |
| Active | `true` |

**After saving**: Note each Company Node Code.

---

## Step 1.5 — Treasury Account Master

**App**: Treasury Account Master
**Action**: Create 6 treasury account records

These link GL accounts to the deal structure with purpose, leg role, and accounting path.

| # | Treasury Account Code | Treasury Account Name | General Ledger Account | Accounting Path | Account Purpose | Leg Type | Company Financial Node | Effective From |
|---|----------------------|----------------------|----------------------|----------------|----------------|---------|----------------------|----------------|
| 1 | `TAM-FXFWD-POS-DR` | FX Forward Position Debit | `GL-FX-FWD-ASSET` | `Own Book` | `Position` | `Position Account` | `4W4U-FXFWD-ASSET` | `2026-01-01` |
| 2 | `TAM-FXFWD-POS-CR` | FX Forward Position Credit | `GL-FX-FWD-LIAB` | `Own Book` | `Position` | `Position Account` | `4W4U-FXFWD-LIAB` | `2026-01-01` |
| 3 | `TAM-FXFWD-SETTLE-ZAR` | FX Forward Settlement ZAR | `GL-ZAR-NOSTRO` | `Own Book` | `Settlement` | `Settlement` | `4W4U-FXFWD-SETTLE-DR` | `2026-01-01` |
| 4 | `TAM-FXFWD-SETTLE-USD` | FX Forward Settlement USD | `GL-USD-NOSTRO` | `Own Book` | `Settlement` | `Settlement` | `4W4U-FXFWD-SETTLE-CR` | `2026-01-01` |
| 5 | `TAM-FX-UNREAL-GAIN` | FX Unrealised Gain | `GL-FX-UNREALISED` | `Own Book` | `Income` | `Income Account` | `4W4U-FX-UNREAL-GAIN` | `2026-01-01` |
| 6 | `TAM-FX-UNREAL-LOSS` | FX Unrealised Loss | `GL-FX-UNREALISED` | `Own Book` | `Income` | `Expense Account` | `4W4U-FX-UNREAL-LOSS` | `2026-01-01` |

**Common fields for all accounts**:
| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Company | *(select 4ward4u Company)* |
| Active | `true` |

**After saving**: Note each Treasury Account Code.

---

## Step 1.6 — Financial Node Account Mappings

**App**: Financial Node Account Mappings
**Action**: Create 6 mappings (one per company financial node)

This is the critical mapping that tells the accounting engine: "For this financial node + this accounting path + this leg role, use THIS GL account."

**Common fields for ALL mappings**:
| Field | Value | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Company | `4ward4u (Pty) Ltd` | Select from dropdown |
| GL Account Type | *(auto-filled)* | Derived from GL Account |
| Financial Node Code | *(auto-filled)* | Derived from Company FinNode |
| GL Code (cache) | *(auto-filled)* | Derived from GL Account |
| Effective To | *(leave blank)* | No end date |
| Active | `true` | |
| Approved By | *(leave blank)* | Set when approved |
| Approval Reference | *(leave blank)* | Set when approved |
| Remarks | *(leave blank)* | |

---

**Mapping Definition**

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Company FinNode (L2) | `4W4U-FXFWD-ASSET` |
| Accounting Path | `Own Book` |
| Leg Role | `Position Account` |
| Treasury Account (L4) | `TAM-FXFWD-POS-DR` |
| GL Account (L5) | `GL-FX-FWD-ASSET` |
| Mapping Status | `Active` |
| Effective From | `2026-01-01` |

## Step 1.7 — Nostro Accounts

**App**: Nostro Accounts
**Action**: Create 4 nostro accounts (ZAR, USD, EUR, GBP)

These are the bank accounts used for settlement. The cashflow engine matches projected cashflows to these accounts.

| # | Nostro Code | Nostro Name | Currency | General Ledger Account | Settlement System | SWIFT BIC | Account Number | Nostro Status | Effective From |
|---|------------|------------|----------|----------------------|------------------|-----------|---------------|--------------|----------------|
| 1 | `NOSTRO-ZAR` | ZAR Primary Nostro | `ZAR` | `GL-ZAR-NOSTRO` | `SAMOS` | `4W4UZAJJ` | `4W4U-ZAR-001` | `Active` | `2026-01-01` |
| 2 | `NOSTRO-USD` | USD Primary Nostro | `USD` | `GL-USD-NOSTRO` | `SWIFT` | `4W4UZAJJ` | `4W4U-USD-001` | `Active` | `2026-01-01` |
| 3 | `NOSTRO-EUR` | EUR Primary Nostro | `EUR` | `GL-EUR-NOSTRO` | `TARGET2` | `4W4UZAJJ` | `4W4U-EUR-001` | `Active` | `2026-01-01` |
| 4 | `NOSTRO-GBP` | GBP Primary Nostro | `GBP` | `GL-GBP-NOSTRO` | `CHAPS` | `4W4UZAJJ` | `4W4U-GBP-001` | `Active` | `2026-01-01` |

**Common fields for all accounts**:
| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Company | *(select 4ward4u Company)* |
| Correspondent Bank | *(leave blank for now)* |
| API Connectivity Available | `false` |
| Active | `true` |

---

## Step 1.8 — Activate Instruments for Company

**App**: Instrument Catalogue
**Action**: Use "Activate for Company" action on each instrument

Turn on the instruments this company can trade.

**Instruments to activate**:

| Instrument | Name |
|-----------|------|
| `FX_FWD` | FX Forward |
| `SPOT` | FX Spot |
| `TERM_LOAN` | Term Loan |
| `MONEY_MARKET` | Money Market |

**For each instrument**:
1. Open the instrument in the Instrument Catalogue app
2. Click "Activate for Company"
3. Select 4ward4u Company
4. Confirm

---

## Step 1.9 — Activate the Company

**App**: Companies
**Action**: Click "Activate Company"

Once all the above is configured, activate the company to allow deal capture.

1. Open the 4ward4u Company record
2. Click "Activate Company"
3. Status changes from `Onboarding` to `Live`

---

## Phase 1 Checklist

| # | Step | App | Status |
|---|------|-----|--------|
| 1.0 | Create Business Partner | Business Partners | DONE |
| 1.1 | Create Company | Companies | [ ] |
| 1.2 | Feature Enablements | Company Feature Enablements | [ ] |
| 1.3 | GL Accounts (8) | General Ledger Accounts | [ ] |
| 1.4 | Company Financial Nodes (6) | Company Standard Financial Nodes | [ ] |
| 1.5 | Treasury Account Master (6) | Treasury Account Master | [ ] |
| 1.6 | Node-to-GL Mappings (6) | Financial Node Account Mappings | [ ] |
| 1.7 | Nostro Accounts (4) | Nostro Accounts | [ ] |
| 1.8 | Activate Instruments (4) | Instrument Catalogue | [ ] |
| 1.9 | Activate Company | Companies | [ ] |

**After Phase 1**: The company can capture deals. Proceed to Phase 2 (Client Workflow) or Phase 3 (Deal Capture) depending on whether client book trading is needed.

---

## Reference Table

> Fill in identifiers as you create records. GL accounts are referenced by their GL Code. Other records need their UUID.

| Record | Identifier |
|--------|------------|
| 4ward4u Business Partner | *(already created)* |
| 4ward4u Company | *(UUID)* |
| GL-ZAR-NOSTRO | `GL-ZAR-NOSTRO` |
| GL-USD-NOSTRO | `GL-USD-NOSTRO` |
| GL-EUR-NOSTRO | `GL-EUR-NOSTRO` |
| GL-GBP-NOSTRO | `GL-GBP-NOSTRO` |
| GL-FX-FWD-ASSET | `GL-FX-FWD-ASSET` |
| GL-FX-FWD-LIAB | `GL-FX-FWD-LIAB` |
| GL-FX-PNL | `GL-FX-PNL` |
| GL-FX-UNREALISED | `GL-FX-UNREALISED` |
| CFN-4W4U-FXFWD-ASSET | *(UUID)* |
| CFN-4W4U-FXFWD-LIAB | *(UUID)* |
